Planned 2026-08-31: 8 plans across 7 waves (`06.6.2-01` through `06.6.2-08`), all executed the same day. Plan 08's closing checkpoint — the developer's own real local-browser pass across all 9 checklist groups (viewport/overflow, mobile-nav accessibility, contrast, sidebar, logout, login deep-link, severity, poll lock, keyboard-only) — approved with all 9 passing; two live-investigated observations during the checkpoint resolved as non-blocking (a History mobile-scroll preference already covered by 06.6.3's D-07, and a sidebar-sticky false alarm traced to testing the wrong URL). A post-checkpoint code review (`06.6.2-REVIEW.md`) found 1 Critical (skip-link target not focusable — CR-01) and 4 Warnings (nav-dropdown re-open race, missing hardening response headers, unauthenticated slow-body DoS, duplicate Health-severity DB reads); all 5 fixed via `/gsd-code-review --fix` (`06.6.2-REVIEW-FIX.md`), plus one mechanical `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` correction found while re-verifying. `gsd-verifier` confirmed 17/17 must-haves (`06.6.2-VERIFICATION.md`). One process note: the code-fixer agent's worktree-cleanup step mistakenly fast-forwarded the top-level `main` checkout (not this worktree) by 48 commits during its final merge step — caught immediately, fully recovered with the developer's explicit approval (`main` reset back to `188b00e`/`origin/main`, this worktree's branch fast-forwarded with the 5 fix commits, temp worktree/branch cleaned up), nothing was lost or pushed. Ready for Phase 06.6.3, which depends on this phase's tokens, `page_header()`, sidebar footer region, and severity function.

**Phase 7 (Final On-Glass Verification) — COMPLETE, 2026-08-28**, done independently on `main` (PR #18) while this branch executed the 06.x backlog; merged into this branch just now (no code conflict — only this file). Launched at the developer's explicit request ahead of the 06.x backlog. 07-01 (the phase's only plan) executed across 3 tasks: Task 1 (CLI forcing flags + 02-UI-SPEC.md addendum, commit 0cc4ae1); Task 2 (blocking on-glass verification battery, steps A-H, run interactively with the developer over SSH against the real deployed VPS); Task 3 (findings recorded to hardware/BRINGUP-LOG.md, commit 6bd4126). Real findings, not a rubber-stamp: PT Serif Regular legibility, bezel clipping, state distinction, two-flight composition, and wordmark detail all confirmed "parfait" on real glass with no changes needed. Yellow/Red palette confirmed close enough as-is. Blue/Green, however, failed hard on real glass — D-21's sky-tone values had only ever been screen-confirmed, never checked against real ink — triggering two live deviations, both developer-approved in real time: (1) `panel_format.PALETTE_RGB`'s Blue/Green darkened across two iterations (110,180,225)/(140,195,130) → (45,95,155)/(50,105,65), confirmed "c'est parfait"; (2) the flat D-21 background fill replaced with `dither.dithered_state_background()` (Floyd-Steinberg dither toward White, 2-color palette to avoid a real mid-session bug where the 6-color quantizer picked the wrong ink once Blue/Green were both dark) after the developer found even the corrected flat fill too dark at full-panel coverage (simultaneous-contrast effect) — followed by a third fix, `_paint_text_backing()`, painting a clean undithered plate behind every text run after the dithered speckle hurt legibility behind white text. `server/test_poll_loop.py`'s pinned default-config digest was re-pinned twice (documented, same pattern as the prior D-26-outline re-pin). Full suite green throughout (90% coverage). Session also found and fixed an unrelated production issue: the VPS's `inkframe-poll.timer` (legacy, pre-SkyPane-rename) was left running and failing every 30s since a stale mid-migration state, while the real `skypane-poll.timer` was healthy — the legacy timer was stopped and disabled. Carried-forward open items (see `07-01-SUMMARY.md`): A-02-02-01's real +200ft/min departure threshold still unvalidated against real sensor data (visual path only) [CLOSED 2026-09-02 — validated against real production departure data; see Blockers/Concerns.]; the wall-mounted re-check of ROADMAP criteria 1/4 remains open (D-03, not a blocker); ROADMAP criterion 7's "2-3 alternate Blue/Green theme variants for CFG-01" was only partially fulfilled — the single default "sky" theme was corrected, no additional selectable theme variants were built (open for a future phase if wanted); the `inkframe-*`/`skypane-*` naming drift in this plan's own example commands (documentation only).

06-10 Wave 3 (final plan of the phase's plumbing work) run_once() now reads device_config.json once per cycle and threads the saved theme/tracked-runway into detection and every render.build_canvas() call site, _classify_source_fault() derives CFG-05's alert only from an all-providers-failed diagnostics report (never an empty selection), the D-04 no-detection no-op branch re-renders exactly once on a fault-flag transition (persisted in history.db's meta table, not poll_state.json, to keep save_poll_state()'s single call site unchanged), _should_record_event()/_record_history() write runway_events rows only on a real hex/confirmed_state/corroborated transition with per-cycle meta written every cycle regardless, and _save_to_gallery()/_prune_gallery() cap CFG-11's gallery at 25 entries; all four render call sites restructured to build_canvas()+panel_format.pack_panel() so the gallery hook can archive the pre-pack canvas; test_poll_loop.py grew 8->27 checks including a pinned pre-06-10 byte-identity digest and the device_config.RUNWAYS/geofence runway-id consistency check; all 7 of this plan's requirements (CFG-01/03/05/06/08/11/12) were already marked complete by earlier plans' read/UI side - this plan completes the write/wiring side that makes them genuinely functional); deploy/Caddyfile gains a config-prefixed companion site block and - the load-bearing edit - moves the existing device-protocol block's access log from stdout to a durable rolled JSON file, the sole path to CFG-03's battery-voltage history given the vendored byos_server.py may not be touched (Pattern 6, D-03); deploy/deploy.sh ships and restarts companion/ alongside server/stub-server; deploy/README.md/README.md/ARCHITECTURE.md document the new service, deploy/README.md also recording Assumption A3 (Caddy's JSON header-nesting shape, unverified against a live captured line) as an open item for plan 06-12; **06.6.1-03 executed (2026-08-29), the last of Wave 1's 3 parallel-safe plans, run sequentially on this worktree after 06.6.1-01 (CSS tokens) and 06.6.1-02 (History table density).** `companion/pages/health_page.py`'s D-14 anomaly banner dropped its redundant per-anomaly `<ul>` detail list (the Overview tiles already carry the same information via colour) and `ANOMALY_BANNER_TEXT` was re-pointed at the tiles; `collect_anomalies()` itself is untouched (still the sole gate on whether the banner renders), gaining only a docstring sentence. The battery-trend chart moved out of the Overview `dashboard-grid` (now three tiles: Device check-in, ADS-B pipeline, Corroboration) into its own full-width `.battery-trend-section` card below the grid, via new `BATTERY_SECTION_HEADING`/`BATTERY_SECTION_CLASS` constants and `_battery_trend_section_html()`; `_battery_section()` itself was not touched, minimizing collision surface with sibling phase 06.6 (not yet executed, plans to edit that same function's timestamp formatting). A new `_read_health_inputs()` single-sources the five `_safe_query()` reads `render()` and the new public `health_page.anomaly_active(state_dir, now=None)` both consume, so the two can never disagree; `anomaly_active()` routes through the same section builders and `collect_anomalies()` call `render()` uses, wrapped in a broad fail-closed guard (never raises, mirrors `runway_images_available()`'s Phase 06.4 precedent) since `companion/app.py`'s `page_context()` will call it on every authenticated page render once plan 06.6.1-04 threads it in as `ctx["health_anomaly_active"]` for the Health nav-tab notification dot. `companion/test_status_pages.py` grew 36->42 checks (2 corrected — the abnormal-drop detail-copy assertion inverted, the four-tile check rewritten to three tiles + a positioned battery section — plus 6 new). One deviation worth flagging: the plan's own draft assumed `anomaly_active()` returns `False` for a merely-empty-but-writable directory; verified against real execution that this contradicts the codebase's own pre-existing, documented D-12 "never seen -> warn" freshness default (untouched by this plan) — a genuinely empty `state_dir` already shows the anomaly banner via `render()` itself, predating this plan. The test was corrected to assert no-raise + agreement-with-render() for that case instead of a hard-coded `False`. Full details in `06.6.1-03-SUMMARY.md`. `state.update-progress`'s known percent-corruption bug recurred again this session (wrote `percent: 73`, computed from `completed_phases/total_phases` instead of `completed_plans/total_plans`) — corrected by hand to `percent: 89` (56/63), same pattern documented throughout this file's history. `requirements.mark-complete D-02 D-04` returned both as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent — these are `06.6.1-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched. `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.1"` confirmed `plan_count: 6, summary_count: 3, status: "In Progress"` (plans 04/05/06 remain).
**06.6.1-04 executed (2026-08-30), the first of Wave 2's plans (depends on 01 CSS + 03's `anomaly_active()` signal, both from Wave 1).** `companion/layout.py` gained the phase's icon system: `ICON_IDS` (exactly five: `icon-device`/`icon-pipeline`/`icon-corroboration`/`icon-battery`/`icon-hamburger`, the last defined here even though plan 06.6.1-05 consumes it, so the sprite stays a single write site), `ICON_DEFS_HTML` (one inline `<svg class="icon-defs"><defs>` sprite, outline glyphs, `stroke="currentColor"`, no external asset), `STAT_TILE_ICON_CLASS`, and `icon_html()` (whitelist-then-render, mirroring `status_dot()`/`stat_tile()`'s own state-value discipline — an unknown id renders nothing). `stat_tile()` gained an `icon=None` keyword (byte-identical when omitted); `page_shell()` emits the sprite once per document. `health_page.py`'s three Overview tiles (Device/Pipeline/Corroboration) each carry their own tinted icon; the Battery trend heading carries the battery icon with no tint class (a page section, not a status tile); Airlines confirmed untouched (`git diff` empty). Three pre-existing `test_status_pages.py` svg-count checks were retargeted onto sparkline-specific markers (`<polyline`/`SPARKLINE_DOT_CLASS`) rather than weakened, since the page now also carries four icon `<svg>` instances. `layout._nav_links()` now also yields the route slug; `sidebar_nav(health_alert=)`/`page_shell(health_alert=)` draw a small error-coloured dot plus a visually-hidden " — attention needed" suffix on the Health sidebar link only, gated on a new `ctx["health_anomaly_active"]` computed once per request in `companion/app.py`'s `page_context()` via `health_page.anomaly_active()` (Phase 06.4's `runway_images`-precedent crossing point, so no nav renderer imports `health_page.py` — `companion/pages/__init__.py`'s boundary held) and threaded into all five live authenticated route blocks plus the unreferenced `_render_tab()` helper; the login/404/preview-error `page_shell()` calls (no `ctx` in scope) were deliberately left alone, correctly defaulting the flag off. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 56→62 checks (5 sprite/stat_tile + 1 nav-dot); `companion/test_status_pages.py` grew 42→44 (3 retargeted, 2 new). No deviations — plan executed exactly as written; both `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` bumps computed from each file's real on-disk baseline at execution time, matching the plan's stated 56/42 starting values. `git diff --stat` across all three commits touches exactly the plan's six `files_modified`. **06.6.1-05 executed (2026-08-30), Wave 3's sole plan (depends on 01 CSS + 04 icon sprite, both complete), the last CODE plan of the phase.** `companion/static/nav-dropdown.js` shipped as the project's second JS file — same ES5-safe `"use strict"` IIFE conventions as `battery-trend.js`, reading the open state from `aria-expanded` (single source of truth, so the visual and announced states cannot drift) and closing on Escape (scoped to open-only) with focus returned to the toggle. `companion/app.py` gained `NAV_SCRIPT_ROUTE`/`_NAV_DROPDOWN_JS_PATH`, a shared `_serve_script_file()` body (`_serve_battery_trend_script()` reduced to a one-line delegate, `_serve_nav_dropdown_script()` added as its twin), dispatched pre-auth before any session gate. `companion/layout.py` gained `NAV_TOGGLE_ID`/`MOBILE_NAV_ID`/`MOBILE_NAV_OPEN_CLASS`/`NAV_TOGGLE_LABEL`/`NAV_DROPDOWN_SCRIPT_SRC` and `_mobile_nav_html()` (toggle button + always-server-closed dropdown panel, consuming `_nav_links()` exactly like `sidebar_nav()` does); `_nav_html()` (the horizontal strip renderer) deleted outright — not hidden — with D-00's flexbox root-cause reasoning preserved as a removal-rationale comment, superseding that fix rather than re-tuning it; `page_shell()` restructured so the header holds only the wordmark + hamburger nav, with one deferred `<script src="/static/nav-dropdown.js">` before `</body>`, and the theme picker now lives inside the dropdown instead of a third header copy. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 62→68 checks: two new (Task 1, pre-auth serving + ES5-dialect), three retargeted at the dropdown (two named by the plan, plus a third — plan 06.6.1-04's single-dot notification check, which the plan's own read_first didn't flag as broken but which needed the same one-dot-per-renderer fix its own Task 2 acceptance criteria already specified), and four new (toggle ARIA/label contract, dropdown contents/order, the three-file Python/CSS/JS DOM-contract guard mirroring 06.5-02's battery-chart precedent, and the no-JS floor). Three Rule-1 deviations, all self-inflicted friction in the plan's own literal `<verify>` scripts rather than any behavioral gap: (1) the plan's own Task 2 gate asserts `'_nav_html' not in src`, which is unsatisfiable together with the plan's own required new symbol name `_mobile_nav_html` (which necessarily contains that substring) — verified the real intent (`'def _nav_html(active):' not in src`) instead; (2) Task 3's own `doc.index(L.MOBILE_NAV_ID)` naively matches the toggle's `aria-controls` attribute (which must legitimately equal the panel's id) before the panel's own `id=` attribute, pulling the toggle's `aria-hidden`-carrying icon into the "panel" slice and tripping the literal `'hidden' not in panel` assertion — the actual test-file checks anchor on the more specific `'id="%s"' % MOBILE_NAV_ID` needle instead, and self-verification used the same corrected needle; (3) the pre-existing notification-dot check retarget described above. Full details, including the exact corrected verification commands run, in `06.6.1-05-SUMMARY.md`. `git diff --stat` across all three task commits touches exactly the plan's four `files_modified`; full suite green except the pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest-mismatch (confirmed unrelated, zero `server/` files touched). `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section (same known limitation); `state.record-metric` needed `--flag value` form rather than the workflow doc's positional-looking example (its own `parseNamedArgs` implementation requires `--phase`/`--plan`/etc., not positional args) — noted here in case the doc needs a fix; `state.update-progress`'s call this session again wrote `percent: 73`/lost `current_phase`'s YAML quoting (`completed_phases/total_phases` bug plus quote-stripping, same pattern documented throughout this file's history) — corrected by hand to `completed_plans: 59`, `percent: 94` (59/63), `current_phase: "06.6.1"` restored. `requirements.mark-complete D-00 D-06` returned both as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent (these are `06.6.1-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.1"` confirmed `plan_count: 6, summary_count: 5, status: "In Progress"` (only plan 06 remains — the phase's blocking human real-device verification checkpoint, which the orchestrator handles interactively rather than an executor agent).

**06.6.2-02 executed (2026-08-31), the second of the phase's 8 plans, closing UXA-15.** `companion/app.py` gained `_POLL_LOCK = threading.Lock()` (process-global, mirrors the existing `LOGIN_THROTTLE` singleton shape) and a rewritten `_handle_poll_now()`: `_POLL_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False)` is the first statement, and the entire cooldown-check → `run_once()` → `mark_poll_triggered()` sequence is wrapped in `try`/`finally: _POLL_LOCK.release()` so a losing concurrent request gets an immediate, honest new `poll_already_running` redirect (never a queued/staggered `run_once()` call) and a failed poll still releases the lock for the next attempt. `companion/pages/config_page.py` gained the matching `FLASH_POLL_ALREADY_RUNNING` constant (distinct from `FLASH_POLL_COOLDOWN` — "executing right now" vs. "triggered recently"), `POLL_SUBMIT_PENDING_TEXT = "Polling…"`, and `_poll_submit_script()` — a new ES5-safe, `_js_literal()`-gated IIFE on the zero-cooldown branch that disables the trigger button and swaps its label on submit, purely cosmetic (the lock is the real boundary, documented as such in the docstring); the zero-cooldown branch now also carries `id="poll-trigger-btn"`, matching the disabled branch's existing id. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 69→70 checks: a genuine two-thread concurrent-request test (both threads block on a shared `threading.Event`, released together, against a fresh session with zero cooldown) proving exactly one of two overlapping `POST /poll-now` requests gets `poll_already_running` — not merely asserted by reading the source. `companion/test_config_page.py` grew 37→38: one new check pins `_poll_submit_script()`'s sink-safety/`_js_literal()` discipline. One Rule-1 deviation: two `test_config_page.py` checks (one pre-existing, one this plan's own new required check) used a bare `"disabled" in rendered` whole-document substring search, which false-positived against `_poll_submit_script()`'s own legitimate `btn.disabled = true;` JS property name — both rescoped to a `<button ...>`-tag-scoped regex instead. Full details in `06.6.2-02-SUMMARY.md`. `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch documented throughout this file's history (confirmed unrelated — zero `server/` files touched by this plan's three commits). `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section (same known limitation documented throughout this file's history); `state.update-progress` correctly computed `percent: 92` (65/71) this time, but a later `state.record-metric`/`state.add-decision` call re-corrupted it back to `82` (`completed_phases/total_phases` = 14/17, the same recurring bug) — corrected by hand back to `92`. `requirements.mark-complete UXA-15` returned `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent (`UXA-15` is a `06.6.1-UX-AUDIT.md` finding ID, not a formal REQUIREMENTS.md entry, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched); `UXA-07` — also listed in this plan's frontmatter `requirements` field — was deliberately **not** passed to `requirements.mark-complete` since neither of this plan's two tasks addressed it (it's the programmatic-announcement/`role="status"` finding, genuinely out of this plan's scope; it also appears in `06.6.2-06-PLAN.md`/`06.6.2-08-PLAN.md`, left open for whichever of those actually implements it). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.2"` confirmed `plan_count: 8, summary_count: 2, status: "In Progress"` (plans 03-08 remain).

**06.6.2-03 executed (2026-08-31), the third of the phase's 8 plans (depends on 01's token rework), closing the two structural High-severity findings the audit's own suggested Wave 1 leads with: UXA-01 (desktop-containment half) and UXA-02, fixed jointly with its inseparable no-JS twin UXA-12.** `companion/static/style.css`'s `body` rule gained a guaranteed `overflow-x: hidden;` — the document can never scroll horizontally regardless of any descendant's layout quirk, closing the audit's own measured 287px overflow at 1440x900 with populated History — plus two belt-and-braces defense-in-depth clamps: `.dashboard-main`'s `max-width: 1440px` became `max-width: min(1440px, 100%)` with an added `width: 100%`, and `.data-table-wrap` gained `max-width: 100%` alongside its existing `overflow-x: auto`. Separately, `.mobile-nav`'s clipping half (`max-height`/`overflow`/`transition`) relocated out of the always-applied base rule into a new `.js .mobile-nav`/`.js .mobile-nav--open` rule pair (plus a matching `.js`-scoped `prefers-reduced-motion` override), scoped under a `.js` marker class `companion/static/nav-dropdown.js` now adds to `<html>` as its unconditional first statement — without any JavaScript at all, the base `.mobile-nav` rule alone renders the panel in its natural, unclipped, fully visible flow (UXA-12's floor). `nav-dropdown.js`'s `setOpen()` was rewritten so the native `hidden` attribute genuinely gates the accessibility tree/tab order: opening clears `hidden` before animating in (a hidden element has no box to transition from — synchronous under `prefers-reduced-motion`, via `requestAnimationFrame` otherwise); closing removes the open class immediately and applies `hidden` only once the collapse transition completes (`transitionend`, one-shot listener, or synchronous under reduced motion) — closing UXA-02. `companion/test_companion_app.py`'s `_dropdown_survives_with_javascript_disabled()` was rewritten in place per the plan's own Pitfall 2 guidance (the old assertion — "no `hidden` attribute ever appears" — described the exact pre-fix defect and had to invert to the new correct floor: no `.js` class on the server-rendered `<html>` tag); a new check pins `nav-dropdown.js`'s `.js`-before-lookup string-index ordering and the `hidden`/`transitionend`/`matchMedia`/`prefers-reduced-motion` state machine against `style.css`'s `.js`-scoped rules. `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` 70→71 (71/71 pass). One Rule 1 deviation: the new `nav-dropdown.js` comments initially used backtick-quoted code spans, tripping the file's own pre-existing ES5-safety regression check (no backtick allowed anywhere, guarding against template literals) — backticks removed from the comments, no behavioral change. `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly the plan's three declared `files_modified`; `git diff --stat companion/layout.py` confirmed empty, per the plan's own verification requirement that this plan not touch the panel's server-rendered markup shape. `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch documented throughout this file's history (confirmed unrelated — zero `server/` files touched). This plan's two acceptance-criteria items requiring a real browser (the five-breakpoint `scrollWidth`/`clientWidth` measurement with populated History, and the real accessibility-tree/Tab-order/no-JS-visual check) are explicitly deferred to this phase's closing verification plan, per the plan's own acceptance criteria — flagged in `06.6.2-03-SUMMARY.md`'s `coverage` block as `human_judgment: true`, not gaps in this plan's automated work. `state.update-progress` correctly computed `percent: 93` (66/71); a later `state.add-decision`/`state.record-session` call re-corrupted it back to `82` twice in the same pattern documented throughout this file's history — corrected by hand back to `93` both times. `requirements.mark-complete UXA-01 UXA-02` returned both as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent (these are `06.6.1-UX-AUDIT.md` finding IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.2"` confirmed `plan_count: 8, summary_count: 3, status: "In Progress"` (plans 04-08 remain).

**06.6.2-04 executed (2026-08-31), the fourth of the phase's 8 plans (depends on 06.6.2-03), closing D-15/D-16.** `companion/layout.py` gained `page_header(title, purpose=None, freshness_html=None, action_html=None)`, placed after `empty_state()`: it escapes `title` into `<h1 class="page-title">`, optionally escapes `purpose` into a `<p class="page-header__purpose text-body">`, and interpolates `freshness_html`/`action_html` verbatim when truthy, mirroring `stat_tile()`'s existing "escape the caption, pass already-built content through unmodified" contract; the docstring states the four-parameter signature and order are a literal contract Phase 06.6.3's five per-page redesign plans consume by name. `companion/static/style.css` gained `--font-page-title-size: 30px` (placed between `--font-heading-size: 20px` and `--font-display-size: 24px` in file order, per the plan's own instruction), a new `.page-title` rule (30px serif, distinct from the existing 20px `.text-heading` section-heading role — D-15), plus `.page-header__purpose`/`.page-header` spacing rules. All five authenticated pages' `render()` now open with `layout.page_header("...")` instead of an independent bare `<h1 class="text-heading">...</h1>` literal — Config, Health, History, Airlines, Preview — closing D-16; `config_page.py` and `preview_page.py` each gained `import companion.layout as layout` alongside their existing `from companion.layout import escape_html` line, matching `history_page.py`'s pre-existing dual-import convention. `companion/test_config_page.py` (38→39), `companion/test_status_pages.py` (47→49), and `companion/test_view_pages.py` (28→30) each gained a regression check per touched page pinning the new `<h1 class="page-title">` markup and the absence of the old bare-`<h1 class="text-heading">` pattern. **06.6.2-05 executed (2026-08-31), the fifth of the phase's 8 plans (depends on 02 and 04), closing D-11/D-17/D-21 and UXA-10.** `companion/layout.py` gained five new `icon-nav-*` sprite symbols and `NAV_ICON_IDS` (slug -> icon-id), growing `ICON_IDS` 5->10; `sidebar_nav()` now prefixes every link with its icon and adds `aria-current="page"` to the active link only. New `_logout_form_html()` (the shared `POST /logout` Sign out control — the app's first-ever visible logout control) is grouped with the theme picker inside a new `.sidebar-footer` (desktop) and `.mobile-nav__footer` (mobile) wrapper. New `SKIP_LINK_TARGET_ID`/skip-link markup renders as the literal first element inside `<body>`, `<main>` gained `id="main-content"`, and new `FAVICON_LINK_HTML` serves a local `#B13F16` data-URI favicon with zero external file/route, held as its own constant for 06.6.2-07's `login_shell()` to reuse later. `companion/static/style.css`: `.sidebar-link--active` gained a `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-accent) 12%, transparent)` tinted background plus `var(--radius-control)` corners, left border reduced 3px->2px (kept, not removed, as a secondary cue per the plan's explicit instruction); new `.sidebar-footer`/`.mobile-nav__footer` rules superseded the old theme-form-only bottom-pinning/spacing rules; `.dashboard-sidebar` (>=960px) gained `position: sticky; top: 0` (D-21); new `.skip-link`/`.skip-link:focus` rules. `companion/app.py`'s `/logout` branch moved from `do_GET` to `do_POST` (D-11, after `THEME_ROUTE`, no `require_session()` gate matching that route's own precedent) — a `GET /logout` now 404s, closing T-06.6.2-09 (a stray prefetch/crawler/`<img src="/logout">`-shaped link could previously end an active session). `companion/test_companion_app.py`: renamed the logout-cookie check to `POST /logout`, added a new `GET /logout` 404 check, `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` 71->72 (72/72 pass). Three Rule 1 deviations, all in `test_companion_app.py`/`style.css` comments: (1) three pre-existing regression checks hard-coded the old five-icon cap and the old id-less `<main>` tag shape, both superseded by this plan's own Task 1 changes — corrected to assert the new ten-icon/`id="main-content"` shape; (2) the plan's own Task 2 inline `<verify>` script has a latent duplicate-selector bug (`style.css` has two separate `.dashboard-sidebar {` blocks — base `display: none` and the `@media (min-width: 960px)` override — and the script's unscoped regex always matches the first/base one), independently confirmed correct via a media-query-scoped regex showing `position: sticky; top: 0;` is genuinely present in the right block; (3) explanatory CSS comments initially quoted the literal old selector strings the plan's own removal-assertion checks for, tripping that same check — reworded to prose. `git diff --stat` across all three commits touches exactly the plan's four declared files. `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch documented throughout this file's history (confirmed unrelated — zero `server/` files touched). `state.record-metric`/`state.record-session` both required `--flag value` syntax (positional args silently produced "required" errors) — corrected on retry. `state.advance-plan` again failed with "Cannot parse Current Plan or Total Plans in Phase from STATE.md" (this file has never carried those legacy fields) and `state.update-progress`/`state.record-session` again corrupted `percent` to `82` (`completed_phases/total_phases`) — corrected by hand back to `96` (68/71), same pattern documented throughout this file's history. `requirements.mark-complete D-11 D-17 D-21 UXA-10` returned all four as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent (these are `06.6.2-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.2"` confirmed `plan_count: 8, summary_count: 5, status: "In Progress"` (plans 03, 06-08 remain — 01/02/04 already complete).

**06.6.2-06 executed (2026-08-31), the sixth of the phase's 8 plans (depends on 04 trivially, and on 05 for the pre-existing `_STATUS_DOT_CLASSES`), closing UXA-14/UXA-07.** `companion/pages/health_page.py` gained `overall_severity(device_state, pipeline_state, battery_state, disagreement_warn)` (a pure precedence function — any `error` wins, else any `warn`/disagreement makes it `warn`, else `ok`, with `_source_fault_block()`'s own signal deliberately excluded) and `health_severity(state_dir, now=None)`, the `ctx["health_severity"]` source of truth, fail-closed to `ok`. `companion/layout.py`'s `_health_alert_markup()`/`sidebar_nav()`/`_mobile_nav_html()`/`page_shell()` widened `health_alert` from a boolean to `None`/`"ok"`/`"warn"`/`"error"`, so the nav dot and mobile-nav dot render warn or error styling from one severity value rather than a hardcoded error. New `flash_banner(role=)`/`anomaly_banner(severity=)` choose their ARIA role from real outcome severity, each whitelisted with a safe fallback. `companion/static/style.css` gained `.banner--warn`. `companion/app.py` threaded `ctx["health_severity"]`/`ctx["flash_role"]` through all six call sites that previously used the old boolean `health_anomaly_active`/uniform `flash_banner()` role. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 72→73 checks. Two Rule 1 deviations, both self-inflicted friction in the plan's own inline verify scripts, not behavioral gaps: (1) Task 1's own `<verify>` script cannot pass in isolation before Task 2 lands (an inherent two-task ordering dependency, `render()`'s new 2-argument `anomaly_banner()` call predates Task 2's signature widening) — verified Task 1's real acceptance criteria standalone, then the full suite after Task 2 landed; (2) `companion/pages/__init__.py`'s first-draft docstring wording quoted the literal old ctx-key name, tripping its own verify assertion — reworded to prose. `git diff --stat` across all three commits touches exactly six of the plan's seven declared files (`companion/test_status_pages.py` needed zero edits, confirming the plan's own substring-preservation design). `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch documented throughout this file's history (confirmed unrelated — zero `server/` files touched). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.2"` confirmed `plan_count: 8, summary_count: 6, status: "In Progress"` (plans 07-08 remain).

**06.6.2-07 executed (2026-08-31), the seventh of the phase's 8 plans (depends on 05 and 06), closing UXA-03's remaining scope and D-01/UXA-09's language-policy regression guard.** `companion/layout.py` gained `login_shell(body, ui_theme="auto")`, a deliberately separate, smaller sibling of `page_shell()` — same head structure (doctype, `lang="en"`, `FAVICON_LINK_HTML` reused verbatim from 06.6.2-05) but a `<body>` with only `<div class="login-shell"><div class="login-card">...</div></div>` — no sidebar, no mobile-nav, no icon sprite, no skip link. `companion/static/style.css` gained `.login-shell`/`.login-card` (centered bounded card on desktop, full-width on mobile). `companion/app.py` gained `_validated_next_route(candidate)` — an exact-membership allowlist against `layout.NAV_TABS`'s five routes, never `startswith`/URL-parsed/regex-matched (T-06.6.2-12) — wired into `require_session()` (unauthenticated redirects now carry `?next=<route>` for a `NAV_TABS`-member requested path), `do_GET`'s `LOGIN_ROUTE` branch (validates a `?next=` query value the same way), and `_handle_login_post()` (reads/validates `next` from the form before lockout/password checks, survives every branch, redirects to `next_route or CONFIG_ROUTE` on success — never a raw crafted value). `_login_body()` gained `role="alert"` on the error/lockout paragraph and `autocomplete="current-password"`/`autofocus` on the password field; `_render_login_page()` now calls `layout.login_shell()` instead of `page_shell()`. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 73→79 checks (6 new: deep-link-return round trip, two open-redirect-rejection checks for `https://evil.example`/`//evil.example`, the GET-path next-validation no-hidden-field check, the `login_shell()` markup check, the `page_shell()`/`login_shell()` `lang="en"` agreement guard) plus 6 pre-existing checks updated in place for the new `?next=`-carrying redirect shape (not counted as new). Two Rule 1 deviations: (1) the plan's own action-item text specified a bare `quote(next_route)` call, but Python's `quote()` defaults to `safe='/'` (leaving `/` unescaped) — the plan's own acceptance criteria required the exact `?next=%2Fhealth` wire format, so `quote(next_route, safe="")` was used instead; (2) six pre-existing `test_companion_app.py` checks asserted the old bare `/login` redirect shape, superseded by this plan's own `require_session()` change — updated in place, not counted as new. `git diff --stat` across all three commits touches exactly the plan's four declared files. `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch documented throughout this file's history (confirmed unrelated — zero `server/` files touched). `state.advance-plan` again failed with "Cannot parse Current Plan or Total Plans in Phase from STATE.md" (same known limitation) and `state.update-progress`/`state.record-session` again corrupted `percent` to `82` (`completed_phases/total_phases`) twice in the same session — corrected by hand both times back to `99` (70/71). `state.add-decision` (invoked once without `--summary` to probe its required-args error) wrote a stray `test` placeholder decision to this file's Decisions section before the correct usage was found — replaced with this plan's real two decisions. `requirements.mark-complete UXA-03 D-01 UXA-09` returned all three as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent (these are `06.6.1-UX-AUDIT.md`/`06.6.2-CONTEXT.md` finding/decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.2"` confirmed `plan_count: 8, summary_count: 7, status: "In Progress"` — only plan 08 (this phase's closing verification plan) remains.

`.filter-bar`/`.filter-bar__field`/`.filter-bar__count` (D-20, shared verbatim by History and Airlines via `list-filter.js`'s existing DOM contract); `.data-table-wrap th` sticky header (`position: sticky; top: 0;` with an explicit background and `z-index: 1`, scoped to each table's own scroll container so it never collides with the `>=960px` sticky sidebar); `.copy-btn` (D-23, inherits the global 44x44 `button` floor unmodified), `.preview-frame` (CFG-10's matte frame), and `.gallery-tile a` (closes the anchor-wrapper baseline gap, CFG-11). One Rule 1 deviation, a genuine cross-page regression the UI-SPEC's own literal prose would have introduced, not self-inflicted plan friction: the UI-SPEC's History section specifies the mobile-card/desktop-table breakpoint toggle as the bare unscoped class `.data-table-wrap { display: none; }` below 960px — but `.data-table-wrap` is also used by `layout.data_table()` (consumed by Airlines twice and Health once) and by History's own hand-built table, and Airlines/Health have no `.history-cards` mobile-card counterpart in this phase's scope; implementing the literal unscoped selector would have hidden Airlines' and Health's tables below 960px with nothing rendered in their place. A new `_TextBBoxSpy` helper (monkeypatches `ImageDraw.textbbox()`'s return value, mirroring `_TextSpy`'s shape) lets the check assert on the real measured bbox rather than re-deriving font-fit logic; the new safe-box invariant (neither line's bbox may cross `SAFE_BOX`'s left edge) is strictly tighter than production's own `_assert_within_canvas()`, which only guards the full 1200px canvas. Verdict: right-padding spread across the sample is 5–12px (7px delta) — **no outlier**, both invariants held for every file. The deliberate overflow demonstration needed the offset raised to 870–900 (not the plan's suggested 400, which stayed 13–73px inside the safe box for every combination tried) to trip the new check while production's own guard still passed — proving the new check catches a real, narrower overflow production code does not. `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` 97→98; `git diff server/plane/render.py` empty for this task, confirmed. **Phase 8 (Panel theme rework) — COMPLETE, 2026-08-31, 6/6 plans.** Implemented spike `001-panel-theme-colours`'s findings: White became the new default theme; the text-backing-plate was removed; `adsbdb`'s `callsign_iata` threaded through so the raw ADS-B callsign never displays, replaced by a four-tier content ladder; the previous card's caption grew 16px->20px with a 20px optical offset correction. Plan 08-06's required blocking on-glass verification session (steps A-H, run interactively over SSH against the real deployed VPS) went well beyond its own D-13 minimum: font weight was reopened mid-session and made theme-conditional (Regular on flat themes, Bold on dithered ones) after uniform Bold read "très agressif" on real ink; a real bug was caught and fixed live (a fixed Blue/Green-tuned dither blend was silently applied to every non-white theme, turning flat Black visibly grey); and the THEMES registry was widened from 5 entries to 11 on explicit developer instruction, with Sky retired outright in favour of fully separate pure/light pairs for every Spectra 6 ink (white, black, grey, yellow, yellow_light, red, red_light, green, green_light, blue, blue_light) — this closes ROADMAP Phase 7's success criterion 7 (originally scoped for "2-3 alternate Blue/Green theme variants"), and also closes Phase 7's carried-forward wall-mounted re-check (D-03) via plan 08-06's Step G. Full findings recorded in `hardware/BRINGUP-LOG.md`'s Phase 8 entry and `08-06-SUMMARY.md`. Post-execution code review (`08-REVIEW.md`) found and fixed two genuine, previously-untested bugs isolated to `draw_source_fault_badge()`: its caption ignored the theme's declared weight (contradicting the phase's own on-glass finding, on the default White theme specifically), and its exclamation-mark dot was drawn as a degenerate zero-length line (Pillow paints that as a single, effectively invisible pixel) rather than a visible filled dot — both fixed, with two new regression checks closing the blind spot (`server/test_render.py` `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` 99->101). `gsd-verifier` confirmed 6/6 must-haves (`08-VERIFICATION.md`), flagging only that ROADMAP.md's goal text ("Black, Yellow, Red and the existing Sky theme") is stale relative to the legitimate 11-theme/no-Sky outcome — not a defect. **Phase 9 (Diagonal band theme) — COMPLETE, 2026-09-02, 4/4 plans.** Implemented spike `003-diagonal-band-theme`'s findings: a new dedicated theme family (5 colours — Blue, Blue Light, Green Light, Red, Black — additive to Phase 8's 11, which stay untouched) drawing a diagonal trapezoid band behind the aircraft illustration, a split top label, and a three-tier flight-identifier hierarchy on both cards. Plans 09-01/02/03 (registry, band drawing primitive + top-label split, text hierarchy) executed cleanly with no deviations. Plan 09-04's required blocking on-glass verification session (steps A-E, run interactively over SSH against the real deployed VPS) found real ink disagreeing with the spike's screen preview twice: (1) black text was illegible on every band colour, not just Black as the spike's round 13 fix anticipated — white ink widened from a `band_black`-only exception to unconditional for all 5 band themes, discovered incrementally (Blue found first, Green next, each re-confirmed on glass before generalizing further) and Blue Light's black-ink hypothesis explicitly tried and rejected; (2) the main card's dash rule read too short (24px->48px) and the shared `center_x` anchor moved from the block's top y to its midpoint (the trapezoid's centreline drift was concentrating in the lower two lines). A developer-driven long-name stress test (reusing Phase 7's own fixture) then surfaced two more real bugs beyond the plan's original scope, each requiring explicit developer authorization before being fixed (both out-of-bounds by plan 09-04's own `<in_session_correction_scope>`): the band's three text roles had no shrink-to-fit at all (added `_role_fit_tracked_text_size()` alongside the existing `_role_fit_text_size()`), and the real overflow cause was text fitting against `SAFE_BOX`'s width instead of the diagonal band's own narrower, height-varying width — white text past the band's edge onto White simply vanished rather than clipping, fixed via a new `_band_edges()` helper. A remaining overflow on an artificially extreme fixture led the developer to correctly diagnose it as a data problem, not a rendering one: live-queried against the real `api.adsbdb.com`, confirmed that `municipality` fields for real airports (e.g. Toulon-Hyères, a genuine Orly destination) are `/`-separated compound names — `enrich._primary_city_name()` now reduces these to their first segment project-wide, not just for this theme, fixed in `server/plane/enrich.py` (outside plan 09-04's own declared file scope, also explicitly developer-authorized). All 8 PHASE9-1..8 scope clauses confirmed by `gsd-verifier` (8/8 must-haves, `09-VERIFICATION.md`), reading the real shipped code rather than trusting SUMMARY.md claims, and running all three affected test harnesses directly (`test_config_history.py` 29/29, `test_render.py` 118/118, `test_enrich.py` 52/52). Full findings recorded in `hardware/BRINGUP-LOG.md`'s Phase 9 entry. Carried-forward open items: the frame's mounting state (desk/wall) was not asked about this session, unlike Phase 8's own wall-mounted confirmation; the band-width-aware fit was verified on one band colour/position with long names, not all 5; `_primary_city_name()`'s rule was checked against 2 real airports, not exhaustively. **State at end of this session (2026-08-28), on branch `claude/debug-skypane-0c21a9`:** two more items completed on that branch, both unrelated to Phase 06's companion-app work, merged forward from a session that started as a live-device debugging conversation. Quick task 260828-b0d: corrected an unverified manufacturer citation about panel refresh cadence in `firmware/main/Kconfig.projbuild`, `firmware/main/panel_guard.h`, and `server/poll_loop.py`'s `MAX_STALENESS_S` comment, replacing it with the real Good Display GDEP133C02 datasheet finding (the datasheet's only refresh-frequency guidance is a 24h *minimum* against ghosting, not a maximum-rate wear limit as previously claimed) — zero behavioral/numeric change, proven by pre/post digests. Debug session `illustration-crop-text-margin` (resolved, two passes): the user reported the gap between the aircraft illustration and its flight-info text looked inconsistent across airlines; root cause was `draw_illustration()` anchoring layout to each PNG's full source rectangle rather than its actually-painted (opaque) pixels, while every vendored file carries a soft drop-shadow band the renderer already discards before painting — measured gap varied 17-154px by airline. Pass 1 fixed the vertical text gap (user-confirmed via preview renders); pass 2, requested as a follow-up, fixed horizontal centering (main aircraft up to 7.5px off canvas centre), the previous card's right-alignment (up to 26px between the two aircraft's visible edges), and a previously-unreported previous-card vertical-centering drift (5.5-28.5px, found while re-measuring for pass 2) — all three fixed the same way, via the `IllustrationPlacement.content` tight bbox. Card *sizing* deliberately still derives from the full rectangle so one aircraft's rendered size never depends on which airline preceded it. Neither fix has been confirmed on real Spectra 6 glass yet; both are server-side/comment-only so no firmware reflash is needed once deployed. This also means `server/plane/render.py`'s render output changed again since Phase 06's own digest re-pin (see `06-12`'s CI-fix commit) — a second, independent `_DEFAULT_CONFIG_DIGEST` re-pin landed on `main` for the same reason (real pixel output moved, not a platform artifact this time); reconciled during this merge, see git history for the merged value.

Similarly, two of the plan's literal acceptance-criteria grep counts (`action="/config"` expected to equal 1) no longer match verbatim — the real file has 1 genuine `<form action="/config">` tag plus 2 unrelated prose mentions in 06.2-era docstrings/comments; verified the actual behavioural intent directly (`grep -n '<form'`) instead of the stale literal count. Both are documented as Rule 3 deviations in `06.3-03-SUMMARY.md`, not scope creep. `companion/static/style.css` was not touched (plan 06.3-02 owns it, confirmed via `git diff --stat`). Full suite: `test_view_pages.py` 20/20, `test_config_page.py` 24/24, `test_companion_app.py` 55/55.

**06.3-04 executed (2026-08-28), turning Health and Airlines into the actual dashboards D-02 asks for.** `health_page.py::render()` replaced its four `_section()` calls with four `layout.stat_tile()` calls (Device check-in, ADS-B pipeline, Battery trend, Corroboration) inside one `<div class="dashboard-grid">` under a new "Overview" heading; `_section()` deleted as dead code. `airlines_page.py::render()` replaced its two `page-section` wrappers with two `layout.stat_tile()` calls (Unresolved prefixes, Resolution statistics) under a new "Coverage" heading, hoisting the existing `coverage_status(rows)` verdict to drive the registry tile's border colour; the statistics tile stays accent-neutral (`status=None`). Every section builder on both pages (`_device_section`/`_pipeline_section`/`_battery_section`/`_corroboration_section`/`_registry_section`/`_stats_section`) is untouched — only its call site changed, and the anomaly banner/source-fault block stay full-width above the Health grid. `companion/test_status_pages.py` grew 25->28 checks; one Rule 1 fix along the way (a new Airlines check's `>Coverage<` substring assertion initially double-matched the pre-existing status-dot label of the same text — tightened to the exact `<h2>` heading markup). `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports one pre-existing, unrelated failure in `server/test_poll_loop.py` (42/43, the already-documented `_DEFAULT_CONFIG_DIGEST` re-pin gap — reproduced identically via `git stash` before this plan's commits existed, confirming it predates this plan). `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section ("Cannot parse Current Plan or Total Plans in Phase from STATE.md", same known limitation as prior 06.3 plans) and `state.update-progress`'s known percent-corruption bug recurred once more (returned `completed:48/total:57` correctly in its JSON but wrote `percent: 57` — the `total_plans` value — into the frontmatter instead of the correct 84) — both corrected by hand, same pattern documented throughout this file's history. `companion/test_status_pages.py` 28/28, `companion/test_companion_app.py` 55/55.

**06.3-05 executed (2026-08-28) across two sessions, closing Phase 06.3 (5/5 plans complete).** Task 1 (prior session) ran the full suite and reconciled `06.3-VALIDATION.md`'s Per-Task Verification Map against the shipped five-plan breakdown, but found the suite red on a `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch and paused, returning two open items for the orchestrator: the suite-red blocker and Task 2's browser visual sign-off. Both were resolved this session per explicit orchestrator-provided resolution. **Item 1:** `origin/main` was merged into this branch (commit `8585f69`), bringing in the previously-missing `aeac512` digest re-pin fix; `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` was re-run and all four companion harnesses stayed green (`test_companion_app.py` 55/55, `test_config_page.py` 24/24, `test_status_pages.py` 28/28, `test_view_pages.py` 20/20). One failure remained in `server/test_poll_loop.py` (a *different* `panel.bin` digest mismatch than the one the merge fixed) — confirmed via investigation to be the same category of pre-existing, environment-specific issue (macOS-local Pillow/FreeType rendering vs. the pinned Linux-CI-computed digest), independently confirmed out-of-scope four times this session (`06.2-01-SUMMARY.md`, `06.3-02-SUMMARY.md`, `06.3-04-SUMMARY.md`, this reconciliation) and validated clean by real GitHub Actions CI on this exact branch's prior PR (#16). Accepted as the documented exception; `nyquist_compliant` flipped to `true`. **Item 2:** the 24-item browser visual sign-off (sections A-G, 900px/960px/960-1200px/1440px+/phone width) was performed via browser automation with DOM/CSS/computed-style inspection (screenshots unavailable this session) against a locally-seeded dataset (12 battery readings, 3 runway_events rows seeded into the scratch state dir's `history_db`, since the empty-database state hides the Battery tile's grid behaviour and History's table-scroll fix under empty-state blocks). **Verdict: APPROVED, all 24 items passed, zero defects.** Full verbatim findings recorded in `06.3-05-SUMMARY.md`. `06.3-VALIDATION.md` finalized: `status: complete`, `nyquist_compliant: true`, both Validation Sign-Off checkboxes ticked. `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.3"` confirmed `plan_count: 5, summary_count: 5, status: "Complete"`. `requirements.mark-complete D-01 D-02 D-03 D-22` returned all four as `not_found`, consistent with 06.3-01's precedent — these are `06.3-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched. **Phase 06.3 (Companion UI visual design and desktop layout pass) is now COMPLETE, 5/5 plans.** `current_phase` advanced to `06.4` (Runway picker — not yet planned), the next actionable phase per ROADMAP.md order.

**06.4-01 executed (2026-08-28), closing Phase 06.4 (1/1 plan complete).** `companion/app.py` gained `_RUNWAY_IMAGE_DIR`, `_runway_image_filename()`/`_runway_image_path()`, and `runway_images_available()` — a registry-bounded, never-raising existence check mirroring `gallery_entries()`'s own `try/except OSError` discipline, iterating `device_config.RUNWAY_IDS` (never `os.scandir()`) so the result can never report an image for a non-registry id — wired into `page_context()` as `ctx["runway_images"]`. `companion/pages/config_page.py`'s `runway_fieldset()` gained an `images_available=()` parameter (default preserves every pre-06.4 single-argument call site): each option is now wrapped in `.runway-option`, the number/heading promoted into a Display-size `.runway-option__number` span, with an `<img>` conditionally emitted only for ids present in the set — this module still performs zero filesystem access. `RUNWAY_IMAGE_ROUTE_PREFIX`/`RUNWAY_IMAGE_ALT_TEMPLATE` are defined once in `config_page.py` and rebound in `app.py`, matching the existing `FLASH_KEY_*` precedent. A new session-gated `/runway-image/{id}.png` route in `app.py` validates the requested id against `device_config.RUNWAY_IDS` **before** any path is built (validate-then-join, T-06.4-02), returning an identical 404 for an unknown id and a missing file; `require_session()` is its first statement, deliberately deviating from `/static/style.css`'s unauthenticated precedent (T-06.4-01) — confirmed by re-running `companion/test_companion_app.py`'s pre-existing unauthenticated-stylesheet check unchanged. `companion/static/style.css` gained `.runway-option`/`.runway-option__number`/`.runway-option__image` rules built only from existing tokens, with a `var(--color-border, var(--color-secondary))` fallback so the rule is valid whether or not Phase 06.3 has landed. `companion/static/RUNWAY-IMAGES.md` (new file) is the D-03 drop-in asset contract. The phase deliberately ships **zero image files** — verified by `ls companion/static/*.png` returning empty at every task boundary — this is D-02/D-03's documented, fully-tested default state, not an omission; the plan's deferred `<human-check>` (real-image browser sign-off) cannot run until the user supplies assets and is correctly left open, not a plan gap. `companion/test_config_page.py` grew 24→34 checks — **not** the plan's stated 15→25: `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT`'s real on-disk baseline was already 24 by execution time (phase 06.2's LED-toggle checks landed between plan authoring and execution, the exact same drift pattern already documented for 06.3-03), so the plan's own increment-by-batch amounts (+4/+3/+3) were preserved against the real baseline instead of its stale literal targets — a Rule 3 deviation, not scope creep, documented in `06.4-01-SUMMARY.md`. `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing failure already accepted as an exception throughout this file's history: `server/test_poll_loop.py` 42/43 (macOS-local Pillow/FreeType rendering vs. the pinned Linux-CI-computed `panel.bin` digest) — confirmed via `git diff --stat` against zero changes under `server/` in this plan's three commits, so it is definitively unrelated to this plan's work. `requirements mark-complete D-01 D-02 D-03` returned all three as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent — these are `06.4-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched. `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.4"` confirmed `plan_count: 1, summary_count: 1, status: "Complete"`. **Phase 06.4 (Runway picker - show runway number and airport map) is now COMPLETE, 1/1 plan.** `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section (same known limitation documented throughout this file's history) and `state.update-progress` recurred its own known percent-corruption bug — both corrected by hand: `completed_plans: 55` (54 + this plan), `percent: 96` (55/57, rounded), `completed_phases: 10` (Phase 06.4 now complete), `current_phase` advanced to `"06.5"` (Interactive battery trend chart — planned, 3 plans, not yet executed), the next actionable phase per ROADMAP.md order.

## Accumulated Context

### Roadmap Evolution

- Phase 37 dependency corrected (2026-09-23, agreed with the developer): no longer depends on Phase 36 as a whole. Wave A (SEC-01..05, SEC-06 except byos binding, SEC-07 env file + `DEPLOY_HOST_KEY` via `env:`, SEC-08) depends only on Phase 32; Wave B (byos `--bind 127.0.0.1` + `IPAddressDeny`/`IPAddressAllow`, byos secret off the command line) depends on Phase 36, which modifies `stub-server/byos_server.py`. Off-box backup destination decided the same day: nightly pull over SSH by the developer's Mac (launchd), with a freshness alert on the companion health page.
- Phases 32–41 added (2026-09-23): remediation of the whole-repository code audit (quality, efficiency, software architecture), ledger in `.planning/audits/2026-09-23-code-audit.md` — 82 findings, each a requirement (TST/FW/HYG/INT/SEC/EFF/ARC/CMP/DOC). The developer asked for 100 % of the findings, low severity included, inside **v1.0** rather than a v1.1. Decisions taken with the developer the same day: per-device enrolment secret (no flash/NVS encryption), full migration of the test suite to pytest (dev-only), comment purge in the source with an English-only rule, CI aligned on the production Python version, no git-history rewrite. Order: 32 → 33 (tests first, so every later phase refactors on a suite that cannot pass without running), 34 firmware in parallel (one hardware session), then 35 comment purge → 36 state integrity → 37 security/ops and 38 efficiency → 39/40 architecture → 41 docs + re-audit. Added via `gsd-sdk query phase.add` (Goal, requirements and success criteria written by hand).
- Phase 22 planned (2026-09-12): 16 plans across 12 dependency waves, covering CFG-25..CFG-31. Full artefact set on branch `claude/companion-comprehensive-audit-zhpt1m`: `22-AUDIT.md` (the ledger), `22-CONTEXT.md` (twelve locked decisions), `22-RESEARCH.md`, `22-UI-SPEC.md` (approved on the UI checker's second pass), `22-VALIDATION.md`, and `22-01`..`22-16-PLAN.md`. Two developer decisions shaped it: sequencing is by dependency wave and never by calendar (D-11 forbids dates, week numbers and day estimates in any plan — the audit's original week-based plan carried fabricated durations), and the mobile navigation is a bottom tab bar, the overlay drawer withdrawn because `references/mobile-navigation.md` already carried a locked "rejected" verdict from real-device testing that the audit had missed (D-10). Waves 6-11 are serialised because every remaining group needs `style.css` and the phase allows one writer per file per wave. **Lesson worth keeping: the plan-check loop ran twice and its whole yield was one class of defect — acceptance greps that evaluate for the wrong reason.** Nine were found across the two passes: one security gate using a negative lookahead `grep -E` cannot parse (it returned 0 even for a line containing an external URL, leaving its threat unmitigated), one gate demanding zero `border-color: transparent` file-wide when twelve unrelated rules legitimately declare it and the plan owned one (the destructive remedy would have reached forward seven waves and removed the declarations a later plan rewrites), and seven more that passed or failed on comment prose, on a pipe that could never match, or on a `grep -rc` printing per-file counts. A gate that passes for the wrong reason gives false confidence; one that fails for the wrong reason pushes someone to act. Both classes are invisible unless every criterion is executed against the live tree, which is now the standing expectation for this project's plans.
- Phase 22 added (2026-09-12): Companion audit round 4 — a comprehensive UI/UX/design-contract/dynamism audit of the companion on `main` at 541d19c (every page and interactive state screenshotted with Playwright at 1280/390 px, light/dark, FR/EN, on a seeded state directory; DOM geometry measured; two read-only code reviews), validated in full by the developer the same day. Ledger in `22-AUDIT.md` (1 P0, 9 P1, 24 P2, 15 P3 plus 24 validated dynamism suggestions D1–D24), evidence report at https://claude.ai/code/artifact/af979b96-c02d-41bc-ad04-9623ff0d143a. Headline: the Display page cannot be saved with JS on (B1 — `form=`-attached fields outside `<form>` never reach `dirty-state.js`; the fallback Save is hidden), a defect the string-based harnesses cannot see because none parses the DOM. Added via `gsd-sdk query phase.add` (Goal written by hand, same CLI gap as Phases 12-16 and 19).
- Phase 19 added: Companion audit follow-through — fix the open findings from 18-AUDIT.md (2026-09-11)

- Phase 16 added (2026-09-07) — "Calendar-linked flight highlighting — a connected calendar sources colour rules automatically", promoted from `.planning/seeds/SEED-003-theme-direction-scope-color-rules-calendar-highlighting.md`. This is the seed's third sub-idea, the one Phase 15's D-01 deferred; it is unblocked because both of its prerequisites are now met — the export format is known from a real supplied export, and the developer relayed the calendar owner's consent. **Generalised at the developer's request from the seed's person-specific framing to "connect your calendar"**, so no person is named in the design. Scoped against real data before promotion, producing three findings that overturn the seed's own assumptions: (1) the CrewWebPlus iCal export is fully structured — `SUMMARY:TO7061 MPL-ORY(+0200)` parses 15/15 — so the parser is small and the seed's "might be an internal duty code" worry does not apply; (2) the flight number is useless as a match key (26% of 300 real cached flights carry a commercial-looking IATA number, and only 11% of Transavia France ones, the dominant Orly carrier and the owner's employer), while origin/destination is populated on 100% of enriched detections and a same-route collision resolves by time — the NCE-ORY rotations sit ~8h apart, and rotating callsigns turn out to be stable per rotation across days, previously undocumented; (3) **the cap** — on the owner's only Orly duty day inside recorded history, none of her three flights were among the frame's 201 detections, one of them missed while the frame tracked other aircraft in the same minute, because the frame shows one aircraft at a time and does not see most movements. The developer chose this design anyway, over a calendar-driven view that would not depend on detection, knowing it marks a flight only when that flight happens to be on screen. Copy must not overstate it. Added via `gsd-tools query phase.add`, which again left ROADMAP.md's top-level `## Phases` bullet untouched (the documented CLI gap recorded for Phases 12-15) — bullet and Goal written by hand.

- Phase 14 added (2026-09-06) — "Resolve an unidentified flight from the gallery lightbox, with coverage gaps as empty cards". Raised by the developer within minutes of Phase 13 shipping (PR #51, merged as `8f45385`), on seeing the real page rather than from any planning artifact: the standalone "Manually resolved prefixes" table reads as dead weight, a Health *Resolve* click was expected to open a pop-up, and unidentified flights were imagined as empty cards in the Airlines grid that open that same pop-up. Investigation confirmed the mismatch is real and was avoidable: `companion/pages/airlines_page.py` **already** carries a shared `<dialog id="panel-lookup-dialog">` (driven by `panel-lookup.js`, shared with History) and the illustration replace-upload form **already lives inside it** — so "click a card, a modal carries the action" is the page's dominant pattern, and Phase 13 shipped a page section beside it. Process cause, recorded deliberately: Phase 13's `/gsd-ui-phase` ran in `--auto` at the developer's request to chain without waiting, its researcher logged 8 unreviewed discretion calls, and `gsd-ui-checker` passed the contract 6/6 because it validates a spec's *internal* coherence, not its fit with the interaction pattern already shipped on the target page — a gap worth closing in the checker itself. The table is agreed to go but must die **by absorption**, since it is currently the only surface carrying D-06's superseded state and D-07's delete control, both of which exist to give the operator a repair path. Three items deliberately left open for `/gsd-discuss-phase 14`: prefix-versus-flight identity on the card label (resolution is per 3-letter prefix, so a card labelled with one example callsign would mislead), display volume (the gap registry caps at 200 against a 43-key gallery), and the JavaScript posture (Phase 13 shipped zero JS; the lightbox is JS-driven, and inheriting that dependency must be a decision rather than a side effect). Phase 14 is expected to be presentation-layer only — every server-side piece it consumes shipped in Phase 13. Work started in a dedicated worktree (`worktree-phase-14-resolve-in-lightbox`) based on `origin/main` after #51 and #52 merged; `worktree.baseRef` was set to `head` in `.claude/settings.local.json` during Phase 13's ship (it had been unset) and left there, which also restores parallel worktree execution that Phase 13 ran without. Added via `gsd-tools query phase.add`, which again did not touch ROADMAP.md's top-level `## Phases` bullet list — bullet and enriched Goal block written by hand, as for Phases 12 and 13.
- Phase 15 added (2026-09-06) — "Per-direction themes, per-flight colour rules and roster-linked highlighting", promoted from `.planning/seeds/SEED-003-theme-direction-scope-color-rules-calendar-highlighting.md` at the developer's request ("Lancement SEED 3"), the last dormant seed with a real design conversation behind it (planted 2026-09-02 from the developer's own description, enriched 2026-09-04 with the named crew case — K Stewart, who works the flights as crew). Scoped *large* by the seed: three features (per-direction theme selection, per-flight colour rules keyed on callsign/hex, roster-linked highlighting) kept in one file because they share a resolution seam ahead of the theme lookup. Promoted whole so `/gsd-discuss-phase 15` reviews the three together, with the split as the first decision — the roadmap entry recommends shipping the seam plus the first two and keeping the roster half deferred behind its two external unknowns (export format, consent) and its consent prerequisite. Investigation before promotion corrected the seed on three points: every one of the 18 themes already has `departing_index == arriving_index` (Phase 8 D-02 plus the retirement of "Sky"), so there is no pair mechanism to reconcile and sub-idea 1 reduces to a second theme selection; a rule should resolve to a theme id rather than a raw colour, so every on-glass-verified ink/weight/band pairing is reused and no new verification is pulled in; and the detection record carries the ICAO24 `hex` but not the registration, so "tail number" matching is hex-only unless the selection dict grows a field. The roster URL would also be the project's first runtime-stored secret — flagged for `/gsd-secure-phase 15`. Added via `gsd-tools query phase.add`, which again did not touch ROADMAP.md's top-level `## Phases` bullet list (the same documented CLI gap recorded for Phases 12 and 13) — the bullet and the enriched Goal block were both written by hand.

- Phase 13 added (2026-09-05) — "Add an illustration for an unidentified flight from the companion web interface", promoted from `.planning/seeds/SEED-005-upload-illustration-for-unidentified-flights-from-the-web-ui.md` at the developer's request, chosen over the four other open seeds (SEED-003 per-theme scope/colour rules/calendar highlighting, AeroDataBox destination lookup, presence-adaptive poll cadence, and the local RTL-SDR backup — the last already recorded as not feasible on the frame's board in `.planning/notes/rtl-sdr-not-feasible-on-frame-board.md`). The selection round also answered a challenge from the developer that seeds were missing: verified they are not — HEAD's `.planning/` is byte-identical to `origin/main`, all ten seed files match, no seed file exists on any other remote branch, and no seed has ever been deleted (the project's own `260902-ipj` convention closes a seed with a `status` field and a dated addendum rather than removal). Unlike Phases 10-12, this seed has **no design conversation behind it at all** — the seed file says so itself, and its (a)/(b) fork (does uploading also resolve the airline via `enrich.py`'s `_AIRLINE_NAME_CORRECTIONS`, or is it image-only keyed on the callsign prefix as a fifth `select_illustration()` fallback tier?) is that file's own framing, never put to the developer. That makes `/gsd-discuss-phase 13` genuinely load-bearing rather than a formality. Two further items must be settled there: how a flight nobody has identified mints an upload key without reopening threat `T-v26-02-01` — `_handle_illustration_replace()` (`companion/app.py:1082`) tests `key` for registry membership *before* a path is built or a byte is read, so the endpoint structurally cannot serve this use case today, and lifting the gate would let a key originate in user input, which the current design forbids; and whether this phase changes `health_page.py:352`'s `_READ_ONLY_NOTE`, whose verbatim promise to the operator ("read-only by design") is decision D-11/D-12 from `06.6.4.1-04` — this phase reopens that decision rather than working around it. Added via `gsd-tools query phase.add`, which once again did not touch ROADMAP.md's top-level `## Phases` bullet list (the same documented CLI gap recorded for Phase 12 and, for `phase.insert`, in the Phase 6 entry below) — the bullet and the enriched Goal block were both written by hand.
- Phase 12 added (2026-09-05) — "Remote display on/off toggle", promoted from `.planning/seeds/SEED-004-remote-eink-display-power-toggle.md` at the developer's request, chosen over the four other dormant seeds. The seed itself scoped this *small*, on the strength of Phase 10 having already built the render state and the poll-loop hold pattern. Investigation before promotion found that half right: those two do carry over, but `quiet_hours_sleep_s()`'s `max(base_sleep_s, remaining)` does not — it derives `remaining` from a window's end time, and a manual toggle has no end time. An open-ended off state needs a *bounded* sleep so the device still wakes to learn it was switched back on, and choosing that bound is a user-facing battery-versus-responsiveness trade-off rather than an implementation detail. That is why this was promoted as a phase (developer's explicit choice between phase-with-discussion, phase-without, and quick task) rather than run as a quick task, and why `/gsd-discuss-phase 12` must settle the bound, the precedence against quiet hours, and the off-state copy before planning. Added via `gsd-tools query phase.add`, which again did not touch ROADMAP.md's top-level `## Phases` bullet list (the same documented CLI gap already recorded for `phase.insert` in the Phase 6 entry below) — the bullet and the enriched Goal block were both written by hand.

- **Renumbering (2026-08-27, user request):** The phase above was first added as Phase 7 by `/gsd-phase add`'s default (append to the end, depend on the immediately preceding phase — then Phase 6: Final On-Glass Verification). The user asked why it depended on Phase 6; told honestly that this was the tool's mechanical default and not a real technical or sequencing decision, they pointed out it also contradicted Phase 6's own stated intent to be "the project's true last step... done once everything else is finished," and asked for the web-interface phase to run first. Offered a lower-cost alternative (a decimal insertion, Phase 5.1, matching the precedent already set by Phase 3.1 — leaves the ~30 existing "Phase 6" references in completed phases' own artifacts untouched) versus the literal full renumber; the user chose the full renumber despite the cost being made explicit. Executed: `06-final-on-glass-verification/` ↔ new phase directory swapped (git mv for the tracked one, plain mv for the still-untracked one), `06-01-PLAN.md` → `07-01-PLAN.md` with its own frontmatter/threat-IDs/note-ID renumbered (`T-06-01-*` → `T-07-01-*`, `N-06-01-01` → `N-07-01-01`) and a new "Second Renumbering Note" added alongside its original 2026-08-26 Provenance Note (history preserved, not overwritten — the note now correctly says 03-04 became 06-01 at the time, then 06-01 became 07-01 now). ROADMAP.md fully updated: both phase sections swapped and renumbered in place (each carrying its own "Note on numbering" explaining why), the Phase 3/3.1 sections' live "moved to Phase 6" cross-references corrected to "Phase 7", Depends-on chain updated (new Phase 6 depends on Phase 5, Phase 7 now depends on Phase 6), Progress table and Execution Order line updated. REQUIREMENTS.md's CFG-01..05 references corrected from Phase 7 to Phase 6 throughout. **Deliberately left untouched**, matching this project's own established precedent (STATE.md's history stays append-only; the original Phase 3→6 move likewise left Phase 3's own artifacts alone): `03-VERIFICATION.md`, `03.1-01-PLAN.md`, `03-04-PLAN.md`/`03-04-SUMMARY.md`, and the `260827-hyy-*` quick-task files — these are point-in-time records of already-completed phases, accurate as of when they were written, and rewriting them would corrupt the historical record (e.g. `03.1-01-PLAN.md` cites the literal shell command `grep -c 'Phase 6' .planning/ROADMAP.md` it actually ran on 2026-08-27 — changing the string would misrepresent what was verified that day). An assistant session initially told the user ~8 files needed rewriting before catching this distinction from the Phase 4/5 swap precedent; corrected before touching those files. Net changed: 1 plan file (identity only, no substantive content), ROADMAP.md, REQUIREMENTS.md, this STATE.md entry, and two phase directory names.
- **Phase 6 completed (2026-08-28), backlog promoted to decimal phases 6.1-6.6:** After 06-12's live verification (90-minute real ADS-B capture, live Caddy-log/network-posture confirmation, developer sign-off checkpoint), `gsd-tools query phase.complete "06"` was run. It has a real bug: it also flipped Phase 7's top-level roadmap checkbox to `[x]` and appended a false "(completed 2026-08-28)" — Phase 7 (Final On-Glass Verification) has not been touched. Caught and corrected by hand (Phase 7's own "## Phase Details" section, including its `Plans: 0/1` line, was untouched by the bug — only the top-level summary line was wrong). Separately, six backlog items — the two pre-existing (999.1 battery status, 999.2 LED toggle) plus four raised during 06-12's sign-off checkpoint itself (999.3 UI visual design/desktop layout, 999.4 runway picker number/map, 999.5 interactive battery chart, 999.6 timestamp/copy clarity) — were promoted via `gsd-tools query phase.insert 6 "..."` (called six times) into decimal phases 06.1-06.6, all inserted between Phase 6 and Phase 7 at the user's explicit request ("before phase 7... call it 6.1"), matching the exact reasoning that already got Phase 6 itself reordered ahead of Phase 7. The raw `phase.insert` CLI call does not touch the top-level `## Phases` bullet list (unlike Phase 3.1's insertion, which did) or run the fuller `insert-phase.md` workflow's `state.patch`/`state.add-roadmap-evolution` follow-up steps (the `state.patch` field names it tries — "Current Phase"/"Next recommended run" — don't match this project's STATE.md format, consistent with every other STATE.md field-matching failure already recorded above) — both done by hand instead: the six phases added to the top-level list between Phase 6 and Phase 7, and this entry substituting for the automated evolution log. The old `## Backlog` section and its six `999.x` placeholder directories (each holding only a `.gitkeep`) were deleted — their content now lives in the promoted phases' ROADMAP.md entries. `total_phases` becomes 14 (was 8, +6 insertions); `completed_phases` becomes 6 (Phase 7's erroneous 7th was the bug just described).
